# Chromium's Linux tab strip and frame: every parameter

Read out of a shallow clone of chromium/chromium on 2026-09-25 by seven readers and seven adversarial verifiers (a Claude Code workflow), for a system (GTK or Qt) theme, non-touch, ChromeRefresh2023. Citations are repo-relative `file:line`. A `(!)` marks a value the verifier corrected. Values are DIP unless marked px. This is the reference the `chrome-strip` crate and its tests are held against; what NativeTerm's WezTerm implements, and what it leaves out, is in `chrome-strip-parity.md`.

Added by the verifier:

- region.insets = Insets() (0 DIP): no SetBorder/kInternalPaddingKey on the horizontal region view outside IS_MAC, so GetInsets().top() used for NTB/combo y is 0 — grep of chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/horizontal_tab_strip_region_view.cc (only :133-138 button borders, :657 Mac-only) and tab_strip_region_view.{h,cc} (no matches)
- strip.unfocus_button: absent — features::kTabGroupsFocusing FEATURE_DISABLED_BY_DEFAULT (chrome/browser/ui/ui_features.cc:539), so HorizontalTabStripRegionViewOld creates no unfocus_button_ (horizontal_tab_strip_region_view.cc:180-201) and leading_offset stays 0 (:364-378)
- combo.preferred_size = 28 x 41 DIP: TabStripComboButton has no CalculatePreferredSize override; BoxLayout adds host insets TLBR(6,0,7,0) to the 28x28 child (ui/views/layout/box_layout.cc:200,227-232,246-247; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/shared/tab_strip_flat_edge_button.cc:83-95; horizontal_tab_strip_region_view.cc:636-642)
- layout.tab_bounds_animation_duration = gfx::Animation::RichAnimationDuration(200 ms) (0 when rich animations disabled) for BoundsAnimator moves of tab slot views (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab_container_impl.cc:1141-1147)
- layout.container_min_and_preferred_width: TabStripLayoutHelper::CalculateMinimumWidth = CalculateIdealBounds(available=0).back().right() (all tabs at min: f clamps to 0 in Below domain); CalculatePreferredWidth = CalculateIdealBounds(nullopt).back().right() (all at preferred); both feed TabContainerImpl::GetMinimumSize / CalculatePreferredSize when no animation is running (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab_strip_layout_helper.cc:189-199; tab_container_impl.cc:881-914)
- layout.tab_closing_mode: EnterTabClosingMode(override_width, source) only for kFromMouse/kFromTouch (returns for kFromNonUIEvent); override_available_width_for_tabs_ = override_width if given else current width(); ExitTabClosingMode resets; StartInsertTabAnimation calls ExitTabClosingMode (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab_container_impl.cc:703-731,1216-1217)
- layout.tab_container_bounds: TabStrip::Layout sets tab_container_ and drag_context_ bounds = TabStrip local bounds (full 41-DIP height, full strip width) (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab_strip.cc:2181-2199)
- frame.frame_edge_insets: FrameEdgeInsets(restored) = IsFrameEdgeVisible(restored) ? RestoredFrameEdgeInsets() : Insets(); IsFrameEdgeVisible = UseCustomFrame && (restored \|\| !IsFrameCondensed); Linux RestoredFrameEdgeInsets = ShouldDrawRestoredFrameShadow ? RestoredFrameBorderInsets : Insets() -> top = 10 restored+shadow, 0 otherwise; drives caption button y and top-area-padding (chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/opaque_browser_frame_view_layout.cc:153-156,264-267; chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_frame_view_layout_linux.cc:68-72)
- frame.window_icon_exclusion: none for a tabbed browser — BrowserView::ShouldShowWindowIcon -> WidgetDelegate::ShouldShowWindowIcon, set from ::ShouldShowWindowIcon which returns false when app_uses_tabbed or when kFeatureTitleBar is unsupported (normal tabbed windows); OpaqueBrowserFrameView::ShouldShowWindowIcon additionally requires GetShowWindowTitleBar; therefore MaybeAddAppIconToLayoutParams does not change the leading exclusion (chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_view.cc:588-604,901-902,3933-3940; chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:266-282,497-502)
- frame.shadow_values_inactive: BrowserFrameViewLinux::GetShadowValues(false) uses Emphasis::kMedium elevation 2 -> key offset (0,2) blur 8 alpha 0x3d, ambient offset 0 blur 4 alpha 0x1f (used only for painting the restored frame border when !ShouldPaintAsActive; layout insets always use active elevation 16) (chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_frame_view_linux.cc:64-69,71-84; ui/views/layout/layout_provider.cc:250-263; ui/gfx/shadow_value.cc:105-129)
- frame.caption_spacing_nonnative: GetWindowCaptionSpacing returns 0 for trailing (leading_spacing=false) spacing, and for leading spacing returns kFrameBorderThickness - kFrameShadowThickness = 3 only when condensed and first button, else forced_window_caption_spacing_ (default <0) or 0 (chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/opaque_browser_frame_view_layout.cc:189-206; opaque_browser_frame_view_layout.h:37,40-41)

Added by the verifier:

- focus_ring: views::FocusRing installed on Tab with TabStyleHighlightPathGenerator -> GetPath(kHighlight, scale 1.0, {}) (DIP rrect, same 28 DIP squircle as the hover/selected fill); halo thickness 2 DIP, default halo inset -1 DIP (kDefaultHaloInset = -0.5 * thickness), SetOutsetFocusRingDisabled(true); colour id kColorTabFocusRingActive\|Inactive = PickGoogleColorTwoBackgrounds(ui::kColorFocusableBorderFocused, kColorTabBackgroundActive\|InactiveFrameActive, ui::kColorFrameActive, kMinimumVisibleContrastRatio 3.0) [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:159-163, tab.cc:417-420, tab.cc:1485-1495, ui/views/controls/focus_ring.h:44-50, ui/views/controls/focus_ring.cc:186-188, chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:751-756, ui/gfx/color_utils.h:41, chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:187-191]
- layout.contents_insets: Tab border = TLBR(6+6=12, 12+8=20, 6+6+1=13, 12+8=20) DIP (kTabVerticalPadding 6 + kTabStripPadding 6; bottom adds kTabstripToolbarOverlap 1; horizontal = bottom corner radius 12 + kTabHorizontalPadding 8); split halves subtract (2+2+2)/2 = 3 DIP on the inner side (int division of 6/-2) [chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:304-314, chrome/browser/ui/layout_constants.cc:113-116, chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/common/horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:408-431, chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:1266]
- layout.min_inactive_width: 32 DIP = kInteriorWidth 16 - separator width 2 + tab overlap 18 (equals kWidthForMaxHoverOpacity) [chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:85-95]
- layout.min_active_width: 56 DIP = kTabCloseButtonSize 16 (non-touch, kRoundedIcons/kDesktopGlowUp disabled -> 16, else 14 but max'd with kFaviconSize 16) + insets 20 + 20; split: minus kTabHorizontalPadding/2 = 4 [chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:62-83, chrome/browser/ui/layout_constants.cc:100-101, ui/base/ui_base_features.cc:506, ui/base/ui_base_features.cc:537-540]
- layout.pinned_width: 64 DIP = kTabPinnedContentWidth 24 + 20 + 20; split: minus 9 (18/2) [chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:49-60]
- hover.frame_inactive_overlay_alpha: under a system theme the end-state hover overlay when the window is inactive is translucent: AlphaBlend result alpha = normalizer = 0x1A*v + 255*(1-v) (dark) or 0x0F*v + 255*(1-v) (light), i.e. the overlay fades from opaque frame colour to a ~10%/6% tint over the frame rather than to an opaque colour [ui/gfx/color_utils.cc:581-610, ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:264-266, ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:307, chrome/browser/ui/color/linux/native_chrome_color_mixer_linux.cc:99-100]
- color.baseline_palette_seed_rule: ref tokens under a GTK/Qt theme come from AddBaselinePalette when key.user_color is unset or user_color_source is kBaseline/kGrayscale; ThemeService sets kBaseline when GetIsBaseline() = !GetUserColor() && !UsingAutogeneratedTheme(); a user colour picked in the theme picker yields kAccent + AddGeneratedPalette(user_color, TonalSpot) and the AddThemedSysColorOverrides branch (HeaderHover dark = Primary30) [ui/color/ref_color_mixer.cc:296-322, chrome/browser/themes/theme_service.cc:753-782, chrome/browser/themes/theme_service.cc:865-869, ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:404-412, ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:66-67]
- color.baseline_neutral_variant: baseline Neutral10/25/99 values depend on features::kChromeDarkNeutrals26 (disabled by default): off -> Neutral10 #1F1F1F, Neutral25 #3C3C3C, Neutral99 #FDFCFB; on -> #191B1F, #363840, #F9FAFC [ui/color/ref_color_mixer.cc:94-121, ui/color/ref_color_mixer.cc:136-164, ui/base/ui_base_features.cc:510]
- stroke.condensed_frame_note: the highlight/fill paths do not change when maximized; only the kHitTest path extends into the 6 DIP top padding (IsFrameCondensed = IsMaximized() \|\| IsFullscreen()) [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/common/horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:120-132, chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_frame_view.cc:200-202]
- separator.height_constant_consumer (resolves reader's unresolved item): LayoutConstant::kTabSeparatorHeight (20 non-touch / 24 touch) is read only by chrome/browser/ui/webui/webui_toolbar/webui_toolbar_layout_css_helper.cc:115 (WebUI toolbar CSS), never by the Views horizontal tab painter [chrome/browser/ui/layout_constants.cc:109-110]
- paint.tab_color_provider_mixer_order: AddChromeColorMixers order = chrome, NTP, omnibox, product specs, tab strip, material chrome/NTP/omnibox/side panel/tab strip (all material ones skipped when key.custom_theme != null), then AddNativeChromeColorMixer LAST (only when key.system_theme != kDefault), then custom_theme->AddColorMixers (no-op for SystemThemeLinux); so under GTK/Qt the native Linux mixer wins for every id it defines [chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixers.cc:71-96, chrome/browser/ui/color/linux/native_chrome_color_mixer_linux.cc:69-73, chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:100-108]

## 3. Tab contents

| parameter | value | condition | citation |
|---|---|---|---|
| `code_path.active_tab_implementation` | legacy Tab (tab.cc) + HorizontalTabStripRegionViewOld + top-level NewTabButton(TabStripControlButton); tabs::kTabStripUnification is FEATURE_DISABLED_BY_DEFAULT so common/tab_view.cc + TabViewHorizontalLayout + TabStripView are NOT active | default build, no --enable-features=TabStripUnification | chrome/browser/ui/tabs/features.cc:91; chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/horizontal_tab_strip_region_view.cc:1155-1161; chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_view.cc:979-980 |

ShouldApplyChromeMaterialOverrides(key) = !key.custom_theme && !high_contrast => FALSE => AddMaterialTabStripColorMixer / AddMaterialChromeColorMixer return early. Mixer order (later wins): AddChromeColorMixer(76) -> AddTabStripColorMixer(80) -> material mixers (82-86, skipped) -> AddNativeChromeColorMixer(89, applies iff key.system_theme != kDefault) -> custom_theme->AddColorMixers(92) which for SystemThemeLinux is an intentional NO-OP | system (GTK/Qt) theme | chrome/browser/themes/theme_service.cc:794-799; chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:36-66; chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:100-108; chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:124-133; chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_widget.cc:487-495; chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_provider_utils.cc:147-153; chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixers.cc:70-93; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:15-19; chrome/browser/ui/color/linux/native_chrome_color_mixer_linux.cc:69-75 |
| `tab.height` | 41 DIP = kTabStripHeight = kTabHeight (34 + kTabstripToolbarOverlap 1 = 35) + kTabStripPadding 6. Added by the verifier:

- favicon.theming: TabIcon::UpdateThemedFavicon: if favicon is the default OR should_themify_favicon -> tabs::ThemeFaviconForTab = favicon::ThemeFavicon(src, alternate = kColorToolbarButtonIcon, active_bg = kColorTabBackgroundActiveFrameActive, inactive_bg = kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive): original = CalculateKMeanColorOfBitmap; recolour with CreateColorMask(alternate) iff min contrast(original vs both bgs) < kMinimumVisibleContrastRatio && alternate contrast > original contrast; else if is_monochrome_favicon -> ThemeMonochromeFavicon: mask to kGoogleGrey900 or SK_ColorWHITE, whichever contrasts more with (active ? kColorTabBackgroundActiveFrameActive : kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive). [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/tab_icon.cc:585-602; chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_favicon_theming.cc:14-30; chrome/browser/favicon/favicon_utils.cc:193-226]
- favicon.default_icon: GetNonDefaultFavicon compares against favicon::GetDefaultFaviconModel(kColorTabBackgroundActiveFrameActive) = 16x16 ImageModel choosing GetDefaultFaviconForColorScheme(IsDark(bg)); default favicon is not painted while loading (MaybePaintFavicon). [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/tab_icon.cc:448-452; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/tab_icon.cc:379-388; chrome/browser/favicon/favicon_utils.cc:106-116]
- tab.focus_ring: Tab is focusable ACCESSIBLE_ONLY, FocusRing::Install with TabStyleHighlightPathGenerator = tab_style_views()->GetPath(PathType::kHighlight, 1.0); focus_ring->SetOutsetFocusRingDisabled(true); colour id kColorTabFocusRingActive/Inactive (apparently_active) = PickGoogleColorTwoBackgrounds(ui::kColorFocusableBorderFocused, kColorTabBackground{Active\|Inactive}FrameActive, ui::kColorFrameActive, kMinimumVisibleContrastRatio). [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:151-167; tab.cc:415-420; tab.cc:1490-1494; chrome/browser/ui/tabs/tab_style.cc:187-188; chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:751-756]
- tab.hit_test_mask: Tab::GetHitTestMask = tab_style_views()->GetPath(PathType::kHitTest, device_scale_factor, render_units kDips); SetNotifyEnterExitOnChild(true) so child hover does not end tab hover. [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:481-490; tab.cc:356-358]
- tab.selectable_region_width: GetWidthOfLargestSelectableRegion = min(alert visible ? alert.x : width, close visible ? close.x : width); feeds AlertIndicatorButton click-to-mute threshold (250%/400% of indicator width). [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:1473-1483; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/alert_indicator_button.cc:86-87, 280-281, 417-419]
- close_button.image_layer: TabCloseButton destroys the image container layer normally and re-creates it (SetPaintToLayer, non-opaque) only while an ink-drop layer is attached; label tooltips disabled. [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/tab_close_button.cc:72-73; 155-167]
- tab.preferred_size: Tab::CalculatePreferredSize = (GetTabSizeInfo().standard_width, kTabHeight 35) although the laid-out height is TabStyle::GetStandardHeight() 41; UpdateIconVisibility bails when height < 35. [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:904-908; chrome/browser/ui/layout_constants.cc:102-106; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:1311-1313]
- title.colour.gtk_header_selector: under GTK the inactive-tab text colour comes from GetFgColor(header_selector + " label.title") with header_selector = "headerbar.header-bar.titlebar" when kUseCustomChromeFrame is on, "menubar" when using the native (server-side) frame; inactive frame appends ":backdrop". [ui/gtk/gtk_ui.cc:1131-1136; ui/gtk/gtk_ui.cc:1159-1173; ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:257-260; chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:83-86]
- alert_indicator.paint_opacity: PaintButtonContents multiplies by fade_animation_->GetCurrentValue() (1.0 when no animation); showing_alert_state_ is what is painted (previous state kept while fading out). [chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/alert_indicator_button.cc:246-250; 426-430]
- close_button.contents_bounds_height_caveat: because the LabelButton default border is VH(5,6), the close button's hit region and ink-drop clamp rect are 16 wide x 18 tall (not 16x16); the circle radius 8 is clamped to the centre so the visible hover circle is still 16 DIP. [ui/views/layout/layout_provider.cc:73-74; ui/views/controls/highlight_path_generator.cc:89-96; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab/tab_close_button.cc:183, 205]

| `gtk.colour_extraction` (!) | GetStyleContextFromCss: prepend 'window.background', split selector on whitespace, append each node (every node also gets class 'chromium'; scale = round(device scale)). GetBgColor: apply '* {border-radius:0px; border-style:none; box-shadow:none}', RenderBackground of the whole parent chain into a 24x24 cairo ARGB32 surface, GetAveragePixelValue(frame=false). GetFgColor: GtkStyleContextGetColor; if alpha != 0xFF -> GetResultingPaintColor(fg, bg of same context). GetBorderColor: gtk_render_frame into 24x24, GetAveragePixelValue(frame=true); if not opaque composite over the context bg. GetSeparatorColor: render background+frame at min-size (24 along the axis), GetAveragePixelValue(false). GetAveragePixelValue(frame): sums premultiplied A,R,G,B over all pixels; if sum(A)==0 -> SK_ColorTRANSPARENT; else RGB = sum(c)*255/sum(A) (alpha-weighted un-premultiplied mean) and A = frame ? max pixel alpha : sum(A)/(w*h). All results cached per selector. | GTK system theme | ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:540-559; ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:563-581; ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:585-600; ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:647-667; ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:680-717; ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:368-390 |

Ramps are 12 entries [WHITE, X050, X100..X900, kGoogleGrey900]. src_idx = entry with least contrast to L(color) (lower_bound on decreasing luminances, then compare with the previous entry). one_bg = (bgA==bgB); lum_mid = one_bg ? L(a) : sqrt((L(a)+0.05)(L(b)+0.05)) - 0.05; nearer bg of a luminance = the one on its side of lum_mid. If contrast(src, nearer) < min: targets = one_bg ? [same-side endpoint, other endpoint] : (src outside both ? [same-side endpoint, nearest-to-mid entry, other endpoint] : [nearest-to-mid, same-side endpoint, other]); targ = first target with contrast >= min (else best); step src one toward targ, then first entry toward targ with contrast >= min. Else if contrast > max: targ = entry nearest the nearer bg on src's side, moved toward src until contrast >= min; result = first entry from src toward targ with contrast <= max. Return ramp[res_idx]. |  | ui/gfx/color_utils.cc:70-139; ui/gfx/color_utils.cc:141-321; ui/gfx/color_utils.cc:323-400 |

Added by the verifier:

- hover_card.title_text: FadeLabelView(CONTEXT_TAB_HOVER_CARD_TITLE, STYLE_BODY_3_EMPHASIS) never calls SetEnabledColor, so the colour is ChromeTypographyProvider::GetColorIdImpl -> TypographyProvider::GetColorIdImpl default = ui::kColorLabelForeground = ui::kColorPrimaryForeground: GTK GetFgColor("label"), Qt QPalette::WindowText, default material_ui kColorPrimaryForeground = kColorSysOnSurface (#1F1F1F light / #E3E3E3 dark). Font: size delta of kBodyTextLargeSize, weight SEMIBOLD. (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/hovercard/tab_hover_card_bubble_view.cc:383-385; chrome/browser/ui/views/chrome_typography_provider.cc:147-177; ui/views/style/typography_provider.cc:303-350; ui/color/ui_color_mixer.cc:96; ui/color/material_ui_color_mixer.cc:125; ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:84; ui/qt/qt_ui.cc:644; chrome/browser/ui/views/chrome_typography_provider.cc:100-110)
- hover_card.footer_background: FooterView and its label background use ui::kColorBubbleFooterBackground: GTK = ui::kColorBubbleBackground = GetBgColor(""); Qt = QPalette::Window (kWindowBg); default material_ui = kColorSysNeutralContainer (#F2F2F2 light / #282828 dark). (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/hovercard/fade_footer_view.cc:94, :301; ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:93; ui/qt/qt_ui.cc:656; ui/color/material_ui_color_mixer.cc:45; ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:229-230)
- hover_card.alert_icons: kColorHoverCardTabAlertAudioPlayingIcon = SelectBasedOnDarkInput(ui::kColorBubbleFooterBackground, SK_ColorWHITE, kGoogleGrey800); kColorHoverCardTabAlertMediaRecordingIcon = SelectBasedOnDarkInput(footer bg, kGoogleRed300, kGoogleRed600); kColorHoverCardTabAlertPipPlayingIcon = PickGoogleColor(ui::kColorAccent, footer bg, 3.0) under GTK/Qt; default (guarded material section) = kColorSysOnSurfaceSubtle / kColorSysError / kColorSysPrimary. (chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:738-744; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_chrome_color_mixer.cc:521-524; chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/hovercard/fade_footer_view.cc:47-65)
- tab.discard_ring: kColorTabDiscardRingFrameActive = BlendForMinContrastWithSelf(kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive, 3.0) and ...Inactive = BlendForMinContrastWithSelf(kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameInactive, 3.0) under GTK/Qt (i.e. the frame colour blended toward white/grey900 until 3.0 contrast); default = ui::kColorSysStateInactiveRing (light SetAlpha(Primary20 #062E6F, 0x8C) / dark SetAlpha(Neutral70 #ABABAB, 0x8C)), Inactive = Active. (chrome/browser/ui/color/tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:169-176; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:74-77; ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:299-301)
- tab.group_underline_colours: kColorTabGroupTabStripFrameActive<Color> = SelectBasedOnDarkInput(kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive, base_dark, base_light) and ...FrameInactive<Color> uses kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameInactive; with features::kTabGroupColorRefresh disabled by default the classic palette is used, e.g. Blue: dark kGoogleBlue300 / light kGoogleBlue600, Grey: dark kGoogleGrey300 / light kGoogleGrey700 (per-colour params at chrome_color_mixer.cc:213-315; alternate palette constants in chrome/browser/ui/color/tab_group_color_ids.h). No material or native override, so identical for GTK/Qt/default except that the 'dark input' is the frame colour under system themes. The group colour replaces the tab stroke colour (horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:769) and is TabGroupUnderline::kStrokeThickness thick for an active tab in an unfocused group (:666-670). (chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:139-207, :213-330; chrome/browser/ui/ui_features.cc:69, :104-106)
- tab_strip.scroll_buttons: kColorTabStripScrollButtonBackground = kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive and kColorTabStripScrollButtonIcon = kColorTabForegroundInactiveFrameActive under GTK/Qt; default (non-glass) background = kColorTabBackgroundInactiveFrameActive, icon = ui::kColorSysOnSurfaceSecondary. (chrome/browser/ui/color/tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:215-218; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:65-66, :107)
- tab_strip.combo_button_separator: kColorTabStripComboButtonSeparator = ui::kColorSeparator under GTK/Qt (= ui::kColorMidground: GTK GetSeparatorColor("separator.horizontal"), Qt QPalette::Mid; material_ui maps kColorSeparator to kColorSysDivider but the native mixers override kColorMidground, not kColorSeparator -> under GTK/Qt kColorSeparator = kColorSysDivider from material_ui.cc:140 since it is later than ui_color_mixer; verify at runtime); default = ui::kColorSysDivider (#D3E3FD light / #5E5E5E dark). (chrome/browser/ui/color/tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:210; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:38; ui/color/ui_color_mixer.cc:191; ui/color/material_ui_color_mixer.cc:140; ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:231-232)
- tab_strip.scroll_container_shadow: kColorTabstripScrollContainerShadow = SetAlpha(ui::kColorShadowBase, 0x4D) (kColorShadowBase = dark ? BLACK : kGoogleGrey800), all themes. (chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:771-772; ui/color/ui_color_mixer.cc:192)
- tab.throbber: kColorTabThrobber = ui::kColorThrobber (GTK GetFgColor("spinner"); Qt not set -> material_ui kColorSysPrimary; default kColorSysPrimary #0B57D0 / #A8C7FA); kColorTabThrobberPreconnect = ui::kColorThrobberPreconnect (GTK GetFgColor("spinner:disabled"); else ui::kColorSubtleAccent from core_default = AlphaBlend(accent-with-contrast, kColorPrimaryBackground, 0x47)). (chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_mixer.cc:773-774; ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:225-226; ui/color/material_ui_color_mixer.cc:194; ui/color/ui_color_mixer.cc:255-258; ui/color/core_default_color_mixer.cc:67-69)
- tab.detached_background: kColorDetachedTabBackgroundActiveFrameActive is only defined in material_tab_strip (default theme): dark ? ui::kColorSysSurfaceVariant : ui::kColorSysBase; under GTK/Qt it has no recipe and would resolve to gfx::kPlaceholderColor unless set elsewhere. (chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:27-28)
- tab_search_button.foreground: kColorTabSearchButtonCRForegroundFrame{Active,Inactive} = kColorNewTabButtonCRForegroundFrame{Active,Inactive} under GTK/Qt; default = ui::kColorSysOnSurfacePrimary (#041E49 light / #D3E3FD dark) and ui::kColorSysOnSurfacePrimaryInactive (#1F1F1F / #E3E3E3). (chrome/browser/ui/color/tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:206-209; chrome/browser/ui/color/material_tab_strip_color_mixer.cc:102-105; ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:212-215)
- tab.separator_alpha_animation: the divider is painted with alpha = Tween::IntValueBetween(opacity, 0, 255), opacity = 1 - max(hover_animation_value, adjacent tab's hover value) (adjacent active tabs contribute 0), so the divider fades out during the hover animation rather than switching. (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/common/horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:632-648, :874-878)
- frame.toolbar_button_icon_inactive_default: under the default theme kColorToolbarButtonIconInactive = GetResultingPaintColor(kColorSysStateDisabled, kColorToolbar) rather than the 0x6E alpha used under system themes; relevant because kColorNewTabButtonCRForegroundFrameInactive falls back to it only when the material override is skipped. (chrome/browser/ui/color/material_chrome_color_mixer.cc:535-536; ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:287-289)
- new_tab_button.transparent_bg_via_custom_theme: CustomThemeSupplier::AddColorMixers would also set kColorNewTabButtonBackgroundFrame{Active,Inactive} = SK_ColorTRANSPARENT when SHOULD_FILL_BACKGROUND_TAB_COLOR==0, but SystemThemeLinux::AddColorMixers is a no-op; the native Linux mixer provides the same result. (chrome/browser/themes/custom_theme_supplier.cc:222-228; chrome/browser/themes/theme_service_aura_linux.cc:100-108)

Added by the verifier:

- gtk.css_context_scale: every AppendCssNodeToStyleContext sets gtk_style_context scale = round(LinuxUi display_config().primary_scale) (integer), independent of the canvas scale used in PaintBitmap — ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:542-545, ui/gtk/gtk_util.cc:793-798 (GetDeviceScaleFactor)
- frame.qt_system_theme_path: QtUi::CreateNavButtonProvider and GetWindowFrameProvider return nullptr ('QT prefers server-side decorations'), so with a Qt system theme the frame is BrowserFrameViewLinux (non-native): MD caption buttons 32 DIP wide, MD shadows (elevation 16/2), 8 DIP top radius, 1px 0x26 border; only colours come from the Qt native theme — ui/qt/qt_ui.cc:547-559, chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/browser_frame_view_factory_views.cc:53-77
- frame.system_theme_condition: UsingSystemTheme() on Linux = theme supplier type kNativeX11 (theme_service_aura_linux.cc:152-157); LinuxUiTheme::GetForProfile via LinuxUiGetter (ui/linux/linux_ui.cc:148-153)
- frame.show_window_title_bar: GetShowWindowTitleBar = UseCustomFrame() && !IsFullscreen() && !ViewsDelegate::WindowManagerProvidesTitleBar(IsMaximized()); ShouldShowCaptionButtons = that; IsFrameCondensed adds !ShouldShowCaptionButtons — chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:533-535, 593-595, 874-888
- frame.wm_provides_titlebar: WindowManagerProvidesTitleBar(maximized) = maximized && session != Wayland && desktop env == Unity (static cached) — chrome/browser/ui/views/chrome_views_delegate_linux.cc:36-45, 112-125
- frame.client_view_bounds: CalculateClientAreaBounds(w,h) = Rect(border.left, top, max(0, w - border.width), max(0, h - top - border.bottom)) with border = FrameBorderInsets(false), top = (WCO \|\| unframed \|\| web-app toolbar) ? border.top : NonClientTopHeight(false) — chrome/browser/ui/views/frame/opaque_browser_frame_view_layout.cc:174-187; GetWindowBoundsForClientBounds / GetMinimumSize add NonClientTopHeight(false) + border — 87-113
- frame.non_client_top_height_with_title: only when ShouldShowWindowTitle (popup/app windows, not tabbed): NonClientTopHeight = max(FrameEdgeInsets.top + icon_size + kVerticalPadding(2), DefaultCaptionButtonY(=kFrameShadowThickness 1) + kCaptionButtonHeight(18) + kCaptionButtonBottomPadding(3), web_app_button_height + edge.top + 2) + kContentEdgeShadowThickness(2); icon_size = max(gfx::FontList().GetHeight(), 16) on Linux — opaque_browser_frame_view_layout.cc:26, 36, 56-60, 130-151; opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:517-527
- frame.paint_client_edge: popup windows only (tab strip hidden, toolbar visible, not condensed): two 1-DIP kColorLocationBarBorderOpaque strips at client.x-1 and client.right, height = toolbar height — opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:918-945
- frame.frame_background_inputs: frame_color = kColorFrameActive/kColorFrameInactive by ShouldPaintAsActive (browser_frame_view.cc:253-258, 327-332); theme image IDR_THEME_FRAME[_INACTIVE] only if the theme has a custom image (334-343); overlay likewise; set on FrameBackground each OnPaint (opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:638-652)
- frame.maximized_paint: PaintMaximizedFrameBorder: maximized_top_inset = NonClientTopHeight(true) - NonClientTopHeight(false) (only shifts the theme overlay image); FrameBackground::PaintMaximized(view) uses x=view.x, y=view.y, width=view.width — opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:911-916; ui/views/window/frame_background.cc:99-103, 105-139
- frame.unframed_mode_border_nongtk: BrowserFrameViewLayoutLinux::RestoredFrameBorderInsets also returns Insets(4) in unframed web-app mode before the shadow computation — browser_frame_view_layout_linux.cc:49-53
- frame.frame_type_hint: BrowserWidget frame_type = UseCustomFrame() ? kForceCustom : kForceNative, set in the tree host ctor and on pref change — browser_desktop_window_tree_host_linux.cc:81-83, browser_native_widget_aura_linux.cc:124-127
- frame.prefer_dark_theme_init_property: PlatformWindowInitProperties.prefer_dark_theme = LinuxUiTheme::PreferDarkTheme() (affects _GTK_THEME_VARIANT for server-side decorations) — browser_desktop_window_tree_host_linux.cc:98-107
- x11.hint_widget_size_source: widget_size for input/opaque regions = GetWidget()->GetWindowBoundsInScreen().size() (DIP), scale = device_scale_factor() — browser_desktop_window_tree_host_linux.cc:163-166
- x11.gtk_frame_extents_state_check: UpdateDecorationInsets CHECKs the platform state is kNormal or kUnknown whenever the insets are non-empty — ui/ozone/platform/x11/x11_window.cc:1477-1480
- frame.close_button_ht_component: the close FrameCaptionButton (kMdButton path) is created with ht_component HTMAXBUTTON, not HTCLOSE; the frame hit test still returns HTCLOSE for it — opaque_browser_frame_view.cc:184-187, 396-398
- gtk_frame.header_paint_is_per_paint: the header bitmap (client.width x top_area_height px) is re-rendered on every PaintWindowFrame call (not cached), only the 4fs decoration assets are cached — ui/gtk/window_frame_provider_gtk.cc:392-395, 411-417

Added by the verifier:

- text.label_line_height_linux: Label::Init and Label::CreateRenderText set RenderText min_line_height = Label::GetLineHeight() = max(TypographyProvider line height, font_list.GetHeight()); for CONTEXT_LABEL/STYLE_PRIMARY on Linux that is default_height = font height - 15 + 20 = font height + 5 DIP (chrome_typography_provider.cc:217, 236-238, 253). So the tab title's shaped line is font_height+5 tall with baseline = max(DetermineBaselineCenteringText(font_height+5, fl), round(max_ascent)); Label::GetMinimumSize / preferred height use this line height, though the tab overrides bounds to 35 DIP (ui/views/controls/label.cc:1347-1352, ui/views/controls/label.cc:876, ui/views/controls/label.cc:392-398, ui/gfx/render_text_harfbuzz.cc:1893-1900)
- text.default_font_render_params_effective: default PlatformFontSkia (used by every CONTEXT_LABEL/STYLE_PRIMARY label incl. TabTitle) is constructed with linux_ui->GetDefaultFontRenderParams() = GTK settings only (antialias, hintstyle, rgba) plus FontRenderParams defaults subpixel_positioning=true, autohinter=false, use_bitmaps=false; PlatformFontSkia::GetFontRenderParams only re-queries fontconfig when GetFontRenderParamsDeviceScaleFactor() differs from the value captured in InitFromDetails (set at gtk_ui.cc:459 before capture) or the subpixel force-disable flag flips; thus hinting = gtk-xft-hintstyle and setSubpixel(true) at every DSF for the default font (ui/gfx/platform_font_skia.cc:176-183, 328-354, 401-406; ui/gfx/font_render_params.h:54-74; ui/gtk/gtk_ui.cc:673-676; ui/gfx/font_render_params_linux.cc:81, 297-303)
- text.gtk_fontconfig_params_discarded: GtkUi::InitializeFontSettings stores the fontconfig-resolved FontRenderParams in default_font_render_params_ (gtk_ui.cc:502-503) but no code reads that member (only reset at 1300); GetDefaultFontRenderParams returns the static GTK-only params (673-676)
- scale.x11_font_render_params_dsf: X11ScreenOzone::OnXDisplayListUpdated sets gfx::SetFontRenderParamsDeviceScaleFactor(primary display device_scale_factor) on every XRandR display-list update (ui/ozone/platform/x11/x11_screen_ozone.cc:293-297); primary display DSF = GetDisplayScale(bounds) = matching geometry scale (monitor_scale*font_scale) (ui/base/x/x11_display_util.cc:242-252, 536-543)
- scale.qt_font_description: Qt backend (ui/qt/qt_ui.cc:361-397): family = QApplication font family; if pixelSize()>0: pixel_size = pixelSize, point_size = round(pixel/(96/72)); else point_size = pointSize, pixel_size = round(point*96/72) (constexpr kPointToPixelRatio = 96.0/72.0); style ITALIC from QFont::style; weight = Qt5WeightToCssWeight for Qt5 else raw; render params: antialiasing = !(styleStrategy & NoAntialias), use_bitmaps = styleStrategy & PreferBitmap, hinting = QtHintingToGfxHinting(hintingPreference, fontconfig hinting), subpixel_rendering from fontconfig (QueryFontconfig); QtUi::GetDefaultFontRenderParams caches font_params_ (qt_ui.cc:269-274; ui/qt/qt_shim.cc:261-280). No FontScale() equivalent; Qt display config comes from QtShim::GetDisplayConfig/GetScreenScale (qt_shim.cc:255-258, not fully read)
- text.tab_title_close_gap: title_right for a close button only = close visible left - 2*kTabAfterTitlePadding = -8 DIP (close_x = visible_left - 4 at tab.cc:567, then title_right = close_x - 4 at tab.cc:622); with an alert indicator and close button, alert.x is additionally moved left by kTabAlertIndicatorCloseButtonPaddingAdjustment = 4 when extra_alert_indicator_padding_ (tab.cc:119, 571-580, 1452-1454)
- text.tab_title_animation_easing: title bounds tween uses gfx::Tween::FAST_OUT_SLOW_IN over 100 ms (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.cc:413, 473-479)
- tab.separator.local_offset: separator bounds are translated by -(tab bounds origin * scale) into tab-local px after alignment (horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:502-505); extra_vertical_space is an int (truncated) (487-490)
- text.render_text_display_text_baseline: RenderText::GetDisplayTextBaseline() = GetShapedText()->lines()[0].baseline (ui/gfx/render_text.cc:1557-1560), used by ALIGN_MIDDLE offset (1932)
- text.fontconfig_use_bitmaps: GetFontRenderParamsFromFcPattern also reads FC_EMBEDDED_BITMAP into use_bitmaps (ui/gfx/linux/fontconfig_util.cc:285-289); FC_HINTING false -> HINTING_NONE regardless of FC_HINT_STYLE (291-298)
- assets.pak_selection_rescale_size: ScaleImageSkiaRep target size = ScaleToCeiledSize(rep.pixel_size(), target_scale / rep.scale()) (ui/gfx/image/image_skia.cc:52-53); unscaled() reps are returned as-is (image_skia.cc:255)

- hover_card.hide_trigger.mouse_exit_scope: the mouse-driven hide is TabStrip::OnMouseExited (pointer leaves the whole TabStrip view), not Tab::OnMouseExited (which only ends the tab's own hover highlight); hovering the empty tab-strip area keeps the last tab's card visible; the new tab button / toolbar are outside TabStrip so moving onto them fades the card out. ## Unresolved by the readers

- TabStripComboButton (tab search) exact preferred size in the horizontal strip: TabStripComboButton::CalculatePreferredSize was not read (only GetPreferredSizeForOrientation at tab_strip_combo_button.cc:477-512, which sums visible children = 28 wide). Whether the TLBR(6,0,7,0) border from HorizontalTabStripRegionViewOld::UpdateButtonBorders is added to its preferred height is unverified; the width used for the tab strip left margin is what GetPreferredSize().width() returns at runtime.
- features::kDesktopGlowUp default state (ui/base/ui_base_features.cc) was not checked; it flips IsRoundedIconsEnabled() and therefore kTabCloseButtonSize (16 vs 14) and the NTB/caption icons. Tab minimum active width stays 56 either way.
- GTK-native (system theme) numeric values are all runtime-derived from the theme CSS: caption button image sizes/margins (nav_button_provider_gtk.cc:175-199,384-458), header padding (top_area_spacing_), frame thickness (window_frame_provider_gtk.cc:243-276) and top corner radius (:133-163). No constants exist in code to cite; only the formulas above.
- TabStripRegionView::GetInsets().top() (used for the NTB / combo y): no SetBorder call on the horizontal region view was found in horizontal_tab_strip_region_view.cc (only a Mac-only kInternalPaddingKey property), so it is taken as 0; tab_strip_region_view.h was not read to confirm no border is installed there.
- WindowManagerDrawsTitleBarOverMaximizedWindows() (chrome_views_delegate_linux.cc) details not read; it decides IsFrameCondensed via !ShouldShowCaptionButtons on Unity-like WMs.
- Hover-card, ink-drop, label/font and color-id files listed in the task were not read because they are not layout parameters of the strip; this extraction covers layout only.
- Exact Linux title font family / pixel size / weight: comes from GTK 'gtk-font-name' via LinuxUi::GetDefaultFontDescription() (ui/gtk/gtk_ui.cc:480-508, ui/gfx/platform_font_skia.cc:174-181); not a Chromium constant, so cap height / baseline numbers used by RenderText::DetermineBaselineCenteringText depend on the installed font (Skia fCapHeight, platform_font_skia.cc:456).
- kMinimumContentsWidthForCloseButtons / kTouchMinimumContentsWidthForCloseButtons for the legacy Tab are declared in chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs/tab.h (used at tab.cc:1359-1360); I did not open tab.h to confirm its value equals the 68/100 in common/tab_view_horizontal_layout.cc:28-29 (identical algorithm suggests it does).
- Concrete GTK colour values (label fg, headerbar label.title fg, entry:focus border) are computed at runtime from the GTK CSS theme (ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:44,257-260; ui/gtk/gtk_ui.cc:1109-1113); only the recipes are reported.
- Qt theme path: ui/qt/native_theme_qt.cc / qt_ui.cc were not read; the native_chrome_color_mixer_linux.cc overrides apply to any system_theme != kDefault, but Qt-specific ui::kColorNative* sources were not verified.
- Tab hover card (hovercard/*) and the tab hover glow (glow_hover_controller.cc), separators, backgrounds/shadows were intentionally left out: the task's parameter list covers tab contents only.
- TabStripControlButton uses views::FocusRing default path? FocusRing::Get(this)->SetColorId is called but I did not verify whether the ControlButtonHighlightPathGenerator installed via HighlightPathGenerator::Install is also used by the focus ring (it normally is, via kHighlightPathGeneratorKey) — not confirmed by reading focus_ring.cc path resolution.
- Actual GTK theme CSS values (decoration box-shadow, border, border-radius, headerbar padding/min-height, titlebutton min-size/margin/padding) are read from the running theme at runtime; nothing in the Chromium tree fixes them — only the measurement/derivation algorithms above.
- Qt (ui/qt) frame path: not extracted; QtUi has no WindowFrameProvider/NavButtonProvider in this tree scope check (only GTK implementations were located under ui/gtk); needs a follow-up grep of ui/qt/qt_ui.cc.
- Wayland equivalents of _GTK_FRAME_EXTENTS / opaque / input regions (ui/ozone/platform/wayland) were not read; task asked for the X11 hints.
- TabStrip preferred height (tabstrip_preferred_height feeding GetTopAreaHeight) belongs to the tab-strip domain (chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs) and was not extracted here; kTabHeight = 34 + 1 overlap and kTabStripPadding = 6 exist in chrome/browser/ui/layout_constants.cc:102-108 but the tab strip's GetPreferredSize was not verified.
- Consumers of NavButtonProviderGtk::GetNavButtonHeight in the browser frame were not verified (a recursive grep over chrome/ timed out); it is used by ui/views/window/native_frame_view_layout_linux.cc for non-browser dialogs.
- Qt (ui/qt/*.cc) equivalents of FontScale/gtk-font-name parsing were not extracted; only the GTK backend paths were read.
- RenderText::GetDisplayTextBaseline() (the second term of the ALIGN_MIDDLE y offset) was not read; assumed to be the shaped line's baseline (round(max_ascent)).
- GtkStyleContextGetPadding/Border values used for the window frame asset bounds (window_frame_provider_gtk.cc:425-426) are theme-dependent and were not enumerated.
- CONTEXT_DIALOG_BODY_TEXT_SMALL size delta (used for the default label line height constant) was not read directly; it is assumed to be the 12 px default (delta 0) from chrome_typography_provider.cc:73.
- Concrete RGB of kColorSysStateHeaderHover / kColorSysStateHoverDimBlendProtection / ui::kColorAccent under a GTK or Qt theme: these resolve through sys_color_mixer.cc Ref* tokens seeded from ColorProviderKey.user_color (and GTK accent for kColorAccent); the seed selection path with a system theme (theme_service.cc:753-782, ui/color ref/sys mixers) was not traced.
- Whether GlassFrameService ever reports a glass-eligible frame on Linux (browser_widget.cc:222-228). Not traced; all values above assume IsGlassFrame()==false on Linux.
- GlowHoverController::GetAlpha() / radial_highlight_opacity consumers outside horizontal_tab_style_views.cc were not searched repo-wide; only confirmed unused in the horizontal painter.
- Hover card, bubble border and gfx::ShadowValue files listed in the task were not read: the horizontal tab itself paints no shadow, and hover-card shadows are outside the tab paint parameters requested.
- LayoutConstant::kTabSeparatorHeight (20 non-touch) is defined (layout_constants.cc:109-110) but no consumer in the horizontal tab painter was found; its user was not located.
- kColorTabStripBackground: no such colour id exists in chrome/browser/ui/color/chrome_color_id.h (checked lines 301-318 and 986-1006 plus a grep); the strip area is the frame (kColorFrameActive/Inactive) and the TabStrip's cascading background is kColorToolbar (tab_strip.cc:1162-1163).
- kColorTabCloseButtonFocusRingHovered: does not exist; the ids are kColorTabCloseButtonFocusRingActive/Inactive (chrome_color_id.h:992-993) and were reported instead.
- Whether the tab stroke (kColorTabStrokeFrame*) is actually visible in CR23 depends on the stroke thickness / TabStyle geometry, which was out of scope; only the colour recipe was extracted (painted at horizontal_tab_style_views.cc:761-770).
- Qt palette roles ColorType::kLight/kMidlight/kDark/kShadow map to QPalette::Light/Midlight/Dark/Shadow (qt_shim.cc:179-188) but are not used by any of the tab-strip colours, so they were not detailed.
- Hover-card title label colour: FadeLabelView(CONTEXT_TAB_HOVER_CARD_TITLE, STYLE_BODY_3_EMPHASIS) uses the typography provider's default label colour (chrome typography / ui::kColorLabelForeground path); the exact typography lookup (chrome/browser/ui/views/chrome_typography_provider.cc) was not read.
- GTK high-contrast (ContrastMode::kHigh) path for kColorTabStrokeFrameActive was documented (GetColorWithMaxContrast of toolbar), but no other tab colours have a high-contrast branch on Linux because ShouldApplyHighContrastColors() is compiled to false when USE_GTK (chrome_color_provider_utils.cc:140-141); the non-GTK Linux build (Qt-only, USE_GTK=false) high-contrast branch in chrome_color_mixer.cc:1208-1222 was not evaluated further.
- Concrete RGB values of kColorSysOnSurfaceSubtle (domain/footer text) and kColorSysSurfaceVariant (border stroke) under a GTK/Qt system theme: they resolve through the ref/sys palette (ui/color/sys_color_mixer.cc:163-164, 210-211) whose seed under a system theme was not traced; only the colour-id recipe is given.
- Exact GTK CSS semantics of GetBgColor("") used for kColorPrimaryBackground/kColorBubbleBackground (ui/gtk/gtk_color_mixers.cc:38) were not followed into gtk_util; treat as the GTK window background colour.
- Suppression when a context menu or the tab-search bubble is open: no explicit hover-card hook was found in chrome/browser/ui/views/tabs (tab_search_bubble_host.cc / tab_strip_region_view.cc do not exist at those paths in this checkout); the only mechanism observed is the mouse-press/EventSniffer hide (tab.cc:729-732, controller.cc:182-231). Menu-open-specific behaviour is therefore unverified.
- Default UI font family/size on Linux comes from LinuxUi::GetDefaultFontDescription (GTK gtk-font-name); the 13/12 px sizes are size deltas applied to that base (ui/gfx/platform_font_skia.cc:173-182); the absolute pixel size therefore depends on the GTK font setting and was not computed.
- ShapeContextTokens::kDialogRadius mapping (used by DialogDelegate::GetCornerRadius before AddedToWidget) was not read; irrelevant for the final look because the hover card overrides the radius to 8 DIP in AddedToWidget.
